# knackFunctions V2 migration plan

This document is the per-function plan for moving `knackFunctions.js` to a simplified, modern `knackFunctionsV2.js`. It was produced by analysing every top-level item in the bundle (310 functions, 9 classes and the constants/statements around them) against the Knack JavaScript model and current JavaScript practice, with each verdict independently checked for calling-signature compatibility and for quality.

> **Status (V2 file complete, not yet released):** `knackFunctionsV2.js` now declares every global that `knackFunctions.js` does (365 names, none pending) and loads cleanly as a classic script. Parts are rewritten and parts are copied unchanged:
>
> - **Rewritten without jQuery, with V1/V2 comparison tests (about 190 functions):** the foundation helpers, the form, grid, date, text, detail, clipboard, user, scene and keyword helpers, `KnackNavigator` and `KnackValueResolver` (simplified), `addConnectionIdToRecord`, `buttonToUrl` and the jQuery parts of `CAQuickToggle`. Functions whose signature or return type changed keep their old name as a `@deprecated` shim.
> - **Copied unchanged because V1 was already modern:** the bulk actions family, `KnackAPI`, the multi-form coordinator, the Quill editor, grid truncation, the pickers, the interactive table, the file carousel, the version refresh controller, `KnackError`, the idle watchdog and the confirmation dialog. Their analyses suggest cleanups (dead code, duplicated helpers, a few bugs) that are **not applied yet**.
> - **jQuery that remains, because Knack forces it:** Knack lifecycle events, the Chosen select plugin, and the jQuery UI date and time pickers.
> - **Analysis coverage:** 14 of 27 sections have the full per-function analysis below (C01-C05, C07, C20-C27). The other 13 were ported from a mechanical first pass plus reading the source; the review passes did not complete.

## How to read this plan

- **Every existing global stays.** `knackFunctions.js` loads as a classic `<script>`, so every top-level name is public API and appears in `eslint/knack-functions-globals.json`. V2 keeps each name either as the real implementation or as a compatibility shim.
- **Signature** means more than the parameter list. It covers the global name, parameter order and defaults, accepted input types (selector string, Element, NodeList, jQuery object), return type, sync versus async behaviour, thrown errors, and DOM side effects other code or CSS depend on (class names, ids, data attributes, custom events). A change to any of these counts as a signature change and requires a shim.
- **Shim** means the old name, with the old signature, mapping its arguments to the new implementation and returning the old type. Shims carry `@deprecated Use X` JSDoc and live together in the "Compatibility shims" section at the end of `knackFunctionsV2.js`, so a later V3 can delete them in one place.
- **Waves** stage the work so V1 stays untouched until V2 is complete:
  - **Wave 1 (done):** the oldest layer and the foundation helpers, rewritten with differential tests against V1.
  - **Wave 2 / 3 (ported as-is, cleanups pending):** modules that were already modern or are coupled to a widget, and the large recent classes. They are copied unchanged so behaviour is identical; the cleanups in the per-function notes below are the next step.

## Principles applied

- **Modern JavaScript (ES2020+).** `const`/`let`, default parameters, destructuring, optional chaining, nullish coalescing, template literals, `async`/`await`, `Array.from`/`flatMap`/`Object.entries`, `Intl` for dates and currency, `AbortController` for fetch timeouts, `MutationObserver` instead of polling, `WeakMap` for per-element state, `CSS.escape`.
- **No jQuery.** V2 implementation code uses native DOM only (`querySelector`, `closest`, `classList`, `dataset`, `replaceChildren`, `textContent`, `dispatchEvent`). The only jQuery left is where Knack forces it: Knack lifecycle events are triggered through jQuery so they are bound with jQuery (the `onKnackEvent` primitive), the Chosen select plugin (`chosen:updated`), and the jQuery UI date and time pickers. Functions still accept jQuery objects as inputs. Where a V1 function returned a jQuery collection, the V2 implementation returns native elements under a new name and the old name becomes a shim that wraps the result with `toJQuery()`, because consuming apps may chain jQuery methods on it. Those shims go in V3.
- **Knack's JavaScript model.** Knack is a single-page app with hash routing. Views and scenes re-render on navigation, filtering, sorting, paging and inline edits, so DOM references must be re-acquired on `knack-view-render` / `knack-scene-render` events rather than cached. Data belongs in `Knack.views[viewKey].model` and the view-based records API (`/v1/pages/{scene}/views/{view}/records` with `sort_field`/`sort_order`, `filters` JSON and `rows_per_page`), never the object-based API from the browser. Note: `docs.knack.com` was not reachable from the sandbox used for this analysis, so Knack references here come from prior knowledge of the developer docs and are marked "unverified against live docs" in the per-function notes where that matters.
- **Knack navigator and resolver as standard.** Every V2 function resolves view, field and scene ids with `knackNavigator` and reads or coerces Knack values with `knackValueResolver` instead of private regexes or `Knack.*` lookups. Both classes are ported first (as-is) so everything else can depend on them.
- **Succinct.** One responsibility per function, early returns, no commented-out code, no `//knack` tag comments or stray `};`, no defensive branches for impossible states, shared primitives instead of per-function copies of "resolve this selector-or-element".
- **Consistent error handling.** Report through `errorHandler.handle(error, context, 'functionName')`; never swallow errors silently.
- **Keep JSDoc.** Every V2 function and shim is documented; shims use `@deprecated`.

## Structure of `knackFunctionsV2.js`

1. Header and constants (unchanged names and values).
2. Foundation primitives shared by everything else (see "Shared V2 primitives" below).
3. Domain sections in the same order as V1, so a reviewer can diff section by section.
4. Compatibility shims, grouped at the end, each tagged `@deprecated` and emitting a one-time console warning only when `window.isDeveloper` is true.
5. Nothing is pending any more. If a global is ever added to V1 before release, the structure test fails until V2 declares it or lists it in a `// TODO(v2-wave-N): name` line.

## Release and rollout

V2 is not loaded by any app until it is complete. When it is:

1. Point the build at V2: `builds/copy-global.mjs`, `eslint/generate-globals.mjs`, `server.js` / `simple-server.js` watch paths and routes, `tests/truncateColumnsInGrid.test.cjs` source path, and the Rollup config input.
2. Regenerate `eslint/knack-functions-globals.json` and confirm it is a superset of the V1 list (the check in `tests/v2/globals-parity.test.cjs` does this).
3. Merge to `main`; the existing workflow builds `dist/`, writes the SRI hash, tags a release (use a `feat!:` or `BREAKING CHANGE` commit if any shim is dropped) and notifies GAP-Track and Spot.
4. Consumers bump the pinned tag in `assetLoader.js` (`PROD_PINNED_VERSION`). Developers can trial V2 earlier by pointing the loader's local source at `http://localhost:3001/knackFunctionsV2.js`, which `server.js` already serves as a static file.
5. V3 (later) removes the shims flagged here and can introduce a namespace.

## Testing approach

`tests/v2/` holds a Playwright harness that loads the same function names (and their in-file dependencies, resolved with acorn) from V1 and V2 into two identical pages, with jQuery and stubs for `Knack`, `ktl` and `errorHandler`, runs one scenario in each and compares the results. Run with:

```sh
node --test tests/v2
```

jQuery and Playwright are resolved from the repo's `node_modules`, or from a directory named in `KF_TEST_DEPS`. Chromium is taken from `PW_CHROMIUM` or `/opt/pw-browsers/chromium`.

## Out of scope for this branch

- Call sites in consuming repositories (GAP-Track, Spot, Powderpigs Brain) were not scanned. Before V3 removes any shim, grep those repos for the shim names.
- No V1 code was changed.

## Per-function plan

Columns: **Verdict** (keep / simplify / modernise / dedupe / deprecate / split / remove-dead), **Sig Δ** = any signature change including return type, **Shim** = old name kept as a compatibility shim, **Wave** 1 = rewritten now in `knackFunctionsV2.js`, 2 = next, 3 = port largely as-is.

If a caller relies on `this` (named executedFunction suggests not), use `func.apply(this, args)` with a function expression instead. Risks: API_TIMER (11263) is a promise-based sleep, not a debounce - do NOT dedupe them despite the policy's hint; they are different primitives.; Leading-edge behaviour: none (trailing only); keep. |
| `resolveElement` | 7915–7920 | keep | `resolveElement(input)` | no | no | low | 1 | Already the right shape and name; it just rejects inputs the rest of the library accepts (jQuery, Document). Widening makes it the single resolver every V2 function uses and fixes a real getById bug. Must be defined early in V2 (function declaration, hoisted). Changes: Widen accepted inputs (strictly additive, per policy line 31): `if (input instanceof Node && (input.nodeType === 1 \|\| input.nodeType === 9 \|\| input.nodeType === 11)) return input;` so Document/DocumentFragment contexts work (fixes getById({context: document}) returning null).; Unwrap jQuery and array-likes to the first Element: `if (input.jquery \|\| Array.isArray(input) \|\| input instanceof NodeList) return [...input].find(n => n instanceof Element) ?? null;`.; Use CSS selectors only for strings (unchanged); normaliseToElement (18723) treats strings as HTML markup, so it is NOT a duplicate and must stay separate - rename confusion only. Risks: Widening the return to include Document means callers doing `.closest()` on the result would throw for Document input; today those callers get null and skip. Only getById uses the context path, and it only calls querySelector, so safe - but audit callers in other chunks (getViewRootElement calls .closest on the result: guard with `instanceof Element` there, as it already does).; `$('selector')` previously resolved to null; now resolves to the first element - more permissive, never less. |
| `resolveElements` | 7927–7943 | keep | `resolveElements(input)` | no | no | low | 1 | Good name, good contract, slightly too narrow (no jQuery/HTMLCollection). Widen it and point setVisibility/toggleVisibility/normaliseInputs/the 8220 closure at it so there is exactly one multi-element resolver in V2. Changes: Add jQuery support: `if (input.jquery) return input.toArray().filter(isElement)` (today a jQuery object returns [] silently because it is neither NodeList nor Array).; Accept any iterable/array-like (HTMLCollection, Set, arguments) via `typeof input[Symbol.iterator] === 'function' \|\| 'length' in input` instead of the NodeList/Array pair; HTMLCollection from `.children` currently returns [].; Accept Document/DocumentFragment -> [] stays correct (not Elements); fine.; Collapse to ~8 lines: string -> querySelectorAll; Element -> [input]; iterable -> Array.from(input).filter(el => el instanceof Element); else []. Risks: normaliseInputs (18010) returns arrays unfiltered and wraps unknown objects as `[target]`; if it becomes a shim over resolveElements those non-Element members disappear - its callers (clearInput) only use Element APIs so this is safe, but note it.; Always returns a fresh array (never the caller's array) - keep, callers may mutate. |

### C02 – KnackNavigator (metadata lookups)

Lines 36–797 of `knackFunctions.js`.

| Function | Lines | Verdict | Current signature → proposed | Sig Δ | Shim | Risk | Wave | Plan |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `KnackNavigator` | 40–795 | simplify | `class KnackNavigator { constructor(); normalizePrefixedId(value, prefix); normalizeViewId(viewId); normalizeFieldId(fieldId); normalizeFieldMap(fieldMap = {}); normalizeRawFieldMap(fieldMap = {}); normaliseViewMap(viewMap = {}); getFieldWrapper(viewRoot, fieldId); normalizeRawFieldId(fieldId); normalizeSceneId(sceneId); getViewObject(viewId); isViewForObject(viewId, objectKey, options = {}); findRenderedViewIdForObject(objectKey, options = {}); getViewFieldIds(viewId); getViewColumns(viewId); normalizeViewColumnHeader(headerText); getViewColumn(viewId, columnRef); getFieldIdFromHeader(viewId, headerText); getHeaderFromFieldId(viewId, fieldId, options = {}); getViewColumnIndex(viewId, columnRef); getRenderedViewColumnIndex(viewId, columnRef); getViewColumnSelectors(viewId, columnRef); getViewColumnWidth(viewId, columnRef); getSceneInfoForView(viewId); getCurrentSceneTrail(viewId, options = {}); getCurrentSceneInfo(viewId, options = {}); getFieldMeta(fieldKey); getFieldType(fieldKey); getFieldChoiceOptions(fieldKey); getFieldIdFromLabel(viewId, fieldLabel) }` | no | no | low | 1 | This is 2026 code that already meets the modern-JS targets, so the policy's wave-3 'port as-is' rule applies to its shape, but its two id normalisers are the most-called primitives in the library (~85 call sites outside the chunk) and the singleton is consumed during script evaluation at L1787, L6841-6843 and L6876-6878, so it must physically lead knackFunctionsV2.js from wave 1. The port removes roughly 200 lines of duplicated cache boilerplate, repeated column-key expressions and impossible-state guards without touching any method name, parameter or return type, and fixes two latent correctness issues (permanent null caching, entity mismatch) that currently bite bulk actions on views rendered late or in modals. Changes: L61-70 normalizePrefixedId: stop building `new RegExp` on every call (called ~85 times per render cycle via normalizeViewId/normalizeFieldId). Replace with: `const v = String(value ?? '').trim().toLowerCase(); const digits = v.startsWith(prefix) ? v.slice(prefix.length) : v; return /^\d+$/.test(digits) ? `${prefix}${digits}` : '';` Same inputs/outputs (the current regex is case-insensitive on a lower-cased string, so the 'i' flag is redundant).; L50-59 constructor: collapse ten `_xxxCache` Maps into one private `#cache = new Map()` keyed by `'view::'+id`, `'sceneInfo::'+id` etc, plus `#memo(key, compute)` that replaces the ten identical `if (cache.has(k)) return cache.get(k); ... cache.set(k, v); return v;` blocks at L201, L336-343, L364-376, L395-411, L473-495, L529-574, L629-633, L648-653, L665-682, L715-732. Add public `clearCaches()`.; L100-116 normalizeFieldMap/normalizeRawFieldMap: identical bodies differing only in the mapper; implement via a private `#mapValues(map, fn)` helper: `normalizeFieldMap(m = {}) { return this.#mapValues(m, (v) => this.normalizeFieldId(v)); }`. Keep both public names unchanged (unused in-file but documented for consumers in CHANGELOG L167/L260).; L123-135 normaliseViewMap: move the inline comment at L128 into the JSDoc (`arrays collapse to the first valid id`); drop the redundant `\|\| ''` at L132 (normalizeViewId already returns ''); write as `const first = (v) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]).map((x) => this.normalizeViewId(x)).find(Boolean) ?? '';`.; L141-146 getFieldWrapper(viewRoot, fieldId): keep the method and parameter order but delegate to the global `getFieldWrapper(fieldId, viewRoot)` from line 17197 so there is one DOM implementation: `return viewRoot instanceof Element ? getFieldWrapper(fieldId, viewRoot) : null;` (inside a class method the bare identifier resolves to the global function, not the method).; L197-216 getViewObject: do NOT cache the `null` result (L212-213). Knack.views[viewId] is only populated once a view has rendered and connection-form views in modals are never in Knack.scenes, so a query made before first render currently poisons the cache for the whole page lifetime. Cache only truthy hits. Flatten the double loop with `Knack.scenes?.models?.flatMap((s) => s.views?.models ?? []).find((m) => m.attributes?.key === vid)?.attributes`.; L224-236 isViewForObject: fine; rename local `normalisedObjectKey` or `normalizedValue` so one spelling is used inside the class (prefer UK for new local identifiers, never touch method names).; L244-262 findRenderedViewIdForObject: remove the `typeof document === 'undefined'` guard (L245) - this is a browser-only classic script; same at L420-422 and L437. Tighten `[id^="view_"]` to `.kn-view[id^="view_"]` so form input ids like `view_12-field_3` (Knack's `#view_x-field_y` convention) are not visited; they currently normalise to '' and fall through to `isViewForObject('')` which always returns false, so the result is unchanged but every input element is tested.; L269-277 getViewFieldIds: `[...new Set(fields.map((f) => this.normalizeFieldId(f?.key)).filter(Boolean))]` - one line, same output; normalizeFieldId already tolerates undefined.; L294-324 getViewColumn: three near-identical `find` passes. Add private `#columnFieldId(column)` (= normalizeFieldId(column?.field?.key \|\| column?.id)) and `#columnHeader(column)` (= normalizeViewColumnHeader(column?.header)); the same expression `column?.field?.key \|\| column?.id \|\| ''` is repeated at L302, L306, L316, L345, L371, L433, L480 and in two cacheRef builders (L390-392, L468-470). Resolve `columnRef` once into `{fieldId, header}` whether it is a string or an object, then do a single `columns.find((c) => (fieldId && this.#columnFieldId(c) === fieldId) \|\| (header && this.#columnHeader(c) === header))`. Note: the object branch currently ORs field and header while the string branch prefers field then falls back to header - the single-pass version preserves both because a field match is always tried first via `fieldId &&`.; L385-412 getViewColumnIndex: `findIndex(...) + 1` is already >= 0, so both `columnIndex > 0 ? columnIndex : 0` expressions (L410-411) are dead; return `columnIndex` directly. Use `columns.indexOf(column) + 1` instead of `findIndex((candidate) => candidate === column) + 1`.; L419-452 getRenderedViewColumnIndex: drop `typeof document` (L420) and `instanceof HTMLElement` (L423, L435) guards - `querySelectorAll('th')` can only yield HTMLElements. Replace `headerCell.innerHTML \|\| headerCell.textContent` (L448) - the textContent fallback is unreachable because innerHTML is non-empty whenever textContent is. Either compare `textContent` or make normalizeViewColumnHeader decode entities (see behaviourRisks) so 'R&D' in metadata matches 'R&amp;D' in innerHTML.; L462-499 getViewColumnSelectors: `selectors.filter(Boolean)` (L497) is dead - every pushed string is a non-empty template literal. Use the shared `#columnCacheRef`. Consider returning a copy (`[...uniqueSelectors]`) only if a caller is found mutating it; current callers (L20105, L20112) only `.find` on it.; L507-519 getViewColumnWidth: already modern; `column?.width && typeof column.width === 'object' ? column.width : {}` -> `typeof column.width === 'object' && column.width ? column.width : {}` is the same; keep as is.; L522-575 getSceneInfoForView: 54 lines -> ~20. Three identical `{key, slug}` + cache + return blocks (L535-541, L553-559, L570-575) become one local `const remember = (key, slug) => this.#memo(...)`. The Knack.scenes scan is a single `find` on `flatMap`ped views. Keep the documented preference order (direct scene with slug -> view object scene with slug -> scene scan -> fallback key with slug from scenes) because bulk actions (L6151, L6312, L6695) and version-refresh (L2060) depend on `.key` and `.slug` being populated.; L586-617 getCurrentSceneTrail: already modern. Only change: `String(getIdBeforeSceneSlug(sceneSlug) \|\| '').trim()` -> `getIdBeforeSceneSlug(sceneSlug)` returns a string already (L18849-18857), so the String/\|\|/trim wrapping is redundant.; L630-657 getCurrentSceneInfo: keep. `Number(options?.historyIndex \|\| 0)` -> `Number(options?.historyIndex ?? 0)` is equivalent for the documented inputs; leave the `typeof options === 'number'` convenience branch because the global wrapper at L18757 forwards it.; L664-688 getFieldMeta: prefer Knack's own lookup `Knack.objects?.getField?.(key)?.attributes` (used at L21120 of this file) and fall back to the manual scan; mark unverified against live docs. The `attributes && typeof attributes === 'object'` check (L680) is defensive for an impossible state - `match?.attributes \|\| null` is already object-or-null.; L695-704 getFieldType: drop `_fieldTypeCache`; getFieldMeta is already memoised so the second cache buys nothing. Body becomes `return String(this.getFieldMeta(fieldKey)?.type ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();` - note normalizeFieldId is applied inside getFieldMeta so the '' short-circuit is preserved.; L711-734 getFieldChoiceOptions: replace the `e`/`v`/`seen` loop and the two explanatory comments with `const out = [...new Set(optionEntries.filter((e) => e != null).map((e) => String(e).trim()).filter(Boolean))];` and memoise only when `out.length` (preserve the documented 'retry when empty' behaviour at L716-720).; L742-764 getFieldIdFromLabel: fine; the two `.trim().toLowerCase()` of name/label can share a tiny `const norm = (s) => String(s ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();` local or the class-level `#normText`.; Whole class: use `#private` fields for caches. If the team wants zero risk for consumers that may have reached into `knackNavigator._viewCache`, keep the underscore properties as aliases pointing at the same Map; otherwise `#private` is preferred (no in-file or test code touches them - grep of `_viewCache\|_sceneInfoCache\|...` outside L36-797 returns nothing). Risks: getViewObject null-caching (L212-213): changing this is a deliberate behaviour fix, but any consumer that relied on the second call being cheap for an unknown view now pays a Knack.scenes scan each time; cost is small (scenes * views in-memory).; normalizeViewColumnHeader (L283-290) does not decode HTML entities, while getRenderedViewColumnIndex (L448) feeds it `innerHTML`. A header containing `&`, `<` or non-breaking spaces (`&nbsp;` is common when Knack pads headers) matches today only by field-key/class and silently falls back to 0 for header-only action columns such as 'Edit &amp; Delete'. Fixing it changes which columns resolve - a visible improvement but still a change. CAQuickToggle.matchesViewTarget (L21505-21510) uses the same normaliser on both sides so it is unaffected.; getViewColumns (L269-277) and getViewColumnSelectors (L462-499) return the live Knack metadata array / a cached array instance; a rewrite that returns copies would break any caller that intentionally mutates (none found in-file). Keep returning the same references.; getSceneInfoForView preference order (direct scene -> view object -> scenes scan -> fallback key) must be preserved exactly: bulk-action form flows (L6151) navigate using `.slug`, and the version-refresh controller (L2060) and replicate payloads (L6312) use `.key`; returning `null` where the fallback branch used to return `{key, slug}` would silently disable those features.; `Knack?.views` style optional chaining on an undeclared global still throws ReferenceError. The tests (tests/truncateColumnsInGrid.test.cjs L16, L87, L119) replace `knackNavigator` with a stub precisely because `Knack` is absent in Playwright; V2 must keep `knackNavigator` as a plain `const` so that stubbing pattern (declaring the const before the helper) keeps working.; Caches are never invalidated. Knack metadata is static per page load, so this is safe, but if V2 adds `clearCaches()` it must not be called automatically on `knack-view-render` (that would defeat memoisation on every render).; Private `#fields` would make `JSON.stringify(knackNavigator)` and `structuredClone` behave differently (currently they expose the Maps as empty objects); negligible but note it.; `findRenderedViewIdForObject` returns the first DOM-order match; tightening the selector to `.kn-view` keeps order but would exclude a view root that Knack renders without `.kn-view` (connection-form views use `#connection-form-view` with no `view_` id, so they were never matched anyway).; `getCurrentSceneTrail` treats `sceneEntry.key \|\| sceneEntry.scene_id` as the record id (L600) - the shape of `Knack.views[x].model.view.scene.scenes[]` is unverified against live docs; port the logic verbatim. |
| `knackNavigator` | 797–797 | keep | `const knackNavigator = new KnackNavigator()` | no | no | low | 1 | A one-line singleton with 96 `knackNavigator.` references across the file and 40+ distinct callers listed in the brief; it is pure public API. Nothing to change except placing it first in V2 and documenting it. Changes: Keep as a top-level `const` immediately after the class (class declarations are not hoisted, so the order class -> const must be preserved).; Add a one-line JSDoc (`/** Shared KnackNavigator singleton used by every view/field helper. @type {KnackNavigator} */`) - it is the only global in this chunk without one.; Must appear in V2 before the first load-time consumer: `const knackValueResolver = new KnackValueResolver(knackNavigator)` (L1787) and the `.bind(knackNavigator)` object literals at L6841-6843 / L6876-6878 execute during script evaluation and would throw a TDZ ReferenceError if the navigator were declared later. Risks: Any V2 build step that wraps the file in an IIFE or module would remove this global; consuming apps (GAP-Track, Spot) and the in-repo tests reference `knackNavigator` by bare name.; If V2 ever lazily constructs the singleton (getter on window), the tests' pattern of pre-declaring `const knackNavigator = {...}` before injecting a helper (tests/truncateColumnsInGrid.test.cjs L16/L87/L119) would collide; keep it a plain const. |

### C03 – KnackValueResolver (record value normalisation)

Lines 799–1787 of `knackFunctions.js`.

| Function | Lines | Verdict | Current signature → proposed | Sig Δ | Shim | Risk | Wave | Plan |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `KnackValueResolver` | 803–1785 | simplify | `class KnackValueResolver { constructor(navigator); normalizeFieldId(fieldId); getFieldMeta(fieldKey); getFieldType(fieldKey); getFieldShape(fieldKey); describeFieldValue(record, fieldKey, { preferRaw = true } = {}); getFieldValueMeta(record, fieldKey, { preferRaw = true } = {}); getComparableFieldDisplay(record, fieldKey); buildRequestPayload(source, fieldKeys = []); resolve(record, fieldKey, { mode = 'typed', preferRaw = true, fallback = undefined } = {}); toTypedValue({ rawValue, displayValue, fieldType, preferRaw }); toApiValue({ rawValue, displayValue, fieldType }); toRequestValue({ rawValue, displayValue, fieldType, targetKey }); toDateTimeRequestValue(rawValue, displayValue); toDateTimeRequestValueForFormat(rawValue, targetFormat); _normalizeDateTimeRequestValue(value); _normalizeDateTimeTimeSegment(value); _buildTimeFromClockParts(hoursValue, minutesValue, amPmValue); toConnectionIds(value); isReadOnlyFieldType(fieldType); isStructuredFieldType(fieldType); getDisplayShapeForType(fieldType); getRawShapeForType(fieldType); getWriteShapeForType(fieldType); describeValueShape(value); toStructuredValue(primaryValue, fallbackValue); toBooleanValue(value); toNumberValue(value); toConnectionRef(value); extractResponseFieldValue(recordObj, fieldKey); toStringArray(value); toDisplayString(value, seen = new Set()); toStringSafe(value); normalizeComparableText(value); toJoinedString(value, separator = ', '); extractFieldLabel(recordObj, fieldKey); getRecordLabel(recordObj, fieldKeys) }` | no | no | medium | 3 | Already modern and jQuery-free, so the policy's wave-3 rule applies: port as-is, remove dead code (L1118), collapse duplicated branches (toConnectionRef, toConnectionIds, time padding, shape if-chains) and align formatting. No signature changes and therefore no shims. Changes: L1118 toApiValue: `const displayString = ...` is assigned and never read; delete the line.; L1197 toRequestValue: `return structuredValue === undefined ? undefined : structuredValue;` is an identity; replace with `return this.toStructuredValue(sourceValue, displayValue);`.; L1337, L1348, L1363, L1397: the same `${String(h).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}` template appears four times; extract a private `_toClock(hours, minutes)` (or use the proposed shared `formatClock`) and call it from _normalizeDateTimeTimeSegment and _buildTimeFromClockParts.; L1334-1338 and L1343-1350 _normalizeDateTimeTimeSegment: the numeric-minutes-to-HH:mm block is written twice (once for typeof number, once for a digit string); coerce once with `const minutes = Number(value)` guarded by `/^\d+$/.test(text)` and keep a single 0..1439 branch.; L1575-1595 toConnectionRef: the array branch and the object branch are identical except for `value[0]` vs `value`; collapse to `const item = Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value; if (!item \|\| typeof item !== 'object') return null; const id = this.toStringSafe(item.id); return id ? { id, identifier: this.toStringSafe(item.identifier) } : null;`.; L1401-1418 toConnectionIds: three branches that all do `String(x.id \|\| x \|\| '').trim()`; rewrite as `[].concat(value ?? []).map((item) => this.toStringSafe(typeof item === 'object' ? item?.id : item)).filter(Boolean)` (same output for array, object and primitive inputs).; L871 and L879 getFieldShape: isReadOnlyFieldType(fieldType) is evaluated twice and `${key}_raw` built twice; compute `const readOnly = ...; const rawKey = ...;` once and reuse in both `readOnly` and `write.supported`.; L1425-1436 isReadOnlyFieldType / isStructuredFieldType: array literals are rebuilt on every call (isReadOnlyFieldType is called 4x per getFieldShape path and once per toRequestValue); hoist to `static READ_ONLY_FIELD_TYPES = new Set([...])` and `static STRUCTURED_FIELD_TYPES = new Set([...])` and use `.has(this.toStringSafe(fieldType).toLowerCase())`.; L1443-1497 getDisplayShapeForType / getRawShapeForType / getWriteShapeForType: three if-chains totalling ~55 lines; replace with static lookup objects (`RAW_SHAPES`, and `WRITE_SHAPES = { ...RAW_SHAPES, connection: 'recordId\|array<recordId>', number: 'number', currency: 'number', date_time: 'object\|string', name: 'object{first,middle,last,title}\|string' }`) plus a one-line fallback; getWriteShapeForType keeps the readOnly -> 'unsupported' pre-check. These strings are descriptive only (used by getFieldShape/describeFieldValue) so no runtime branching depends on them.; L1673-1676 toDisplayString: `directKeys` array literal is allocated on every call (toDisplayString is recursive and called from toStringArray/toJoinedString in hot paths such as getRecordLabel); hoist to `static DISPLAY_KEYS`. Also drop `value?.[key]` -> `value[key]` since L1661 already guaranteed `value` is a non-null object.; L1055-1057 resolve and L1035-1036 buildRequestPayload: `record?.[rawKey]` / `source?.[...]` use optional chaining after an explicit `!record` / `typeof source !== 'object'` guard; drop the `?.` (no behaviour change, clearer intent).; L1062-1073 resolve: the mode dispatch is five sequential ifs; a small object map `{ raw: () => rawValue, display: () => displayValue, api: () => this.toApiValue(...), request: () => this.toRequestValue(...), typed: () => this.toTypedValue(...) }` with `(handlers[mode] ?? handlers.typed)() ?? fallback` reads better, but note the `?? fallback` must only apply to undefined for api/request/typed (current code uses `=== undefined`, and `?? ` would also catch null for those modes); keep `=== undefined` semantics for api/request/typed and `??` for raw/display exactly as today.; L1138-1141, L1159-1162, L1204-1207, L1526-1534: the 'trim a string and return undefined when empty' pattern appears four times; use the proposed shared `trimmedOrUndefined(value)` helper (private static or module-scoped inside the class body).; L943-999 getFieldValueMeta: uses trailing commas on object literals (947, 963, 970, 978, 986, 992, 996) while the rest of the class does not; normalise to the file convention. The array branch (955-968) can map through `toConnectionRef` once and reuse `refs` for both the display and raw joins, dropping the separate `joinedValue` recomputation.; L1604-1620 extractResponseFieldValue: replace the two `Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(recordObj, k)` calls with `Object.hasOwn(recordObj, k)` (ES2022; evergreen browsers) and return via `??`-free ternary to preserve the display-before-raw precedence.; L1726-1728 normalizeComparableText: the final `.trim()` is redundant because toStringSafe already trimmed and `\s+ -> ' '` cannot introduce edge whitespace; drop it.; L1764-1784 getRecordLabel: the manual `seen` Set + for loop can become `const parts = [...new Set(keys.map((k) => this.normalizeFieldId(k)).filter(Boolean))].map((id) => this.extractFieldLabel(recordObj, id)).filter(Boolean);` keeping the ' - ' join and the record.id fallback.; L817-828 normalizeFieldId: the fallback branch duplicates KnackNavigator.normalizePrefixedId (navigator is always supplied at L1787). Keep a fallback because `new KnackValueResolver(null)` is reachable by consuming apps, but delegate to the proposed shared `normalisePrefixedId(value, 'field_')` so the regex lives in one place.; L834-849 getFieldMeta / getFieldType: both normalise the key and then the navigator normalises it again (KnackNavigator L694-696, L725-727); simplify to `return this.navigator?.getFieldMeta?.(fieldKey) ?? null;` and `return this.navigator?.getFieldType?.(fieldKey) ?? '';` (identical results for KnackNavigator, which normalises internally).; JSDoc: keep every existing block; add `@private` tags already present on the `_` methods; add `@see` from toConnectionRef to the global `getConnectionRef` alias (L6990) so the duplicate relationship is documented. Risks: toStringSafe (L1712-1720) is called from 150 external sites plus 27 internal ones; its try/catch exists for values whose toString/valueOf throws (hostile objects, revoked Proxies). Any rewrite must keep the catch, keep returning '' for undefined/null, and keep trimming. Do not swap String(value) for a template literal: `${Symbol()}` throws a TypeError whereas String(Symbol()) does not, so a template literal would change which inputs reach the catch.; toTypedValue connection quirk (L1092-1093): zero connected ids yields '' not undefined, and resolve() then returns '' rather than fallback. CAMultiChoiceQuickToggle (L21624) relies on resolve({mode:'typed'}); changing '' to undefined would flip its empty-state handling.; toRequestValue (L1159) uses `rawValue !== undefined ? rawValue : displayValue`, NOT `rawValue ?? displayValue`; a null _raw deliberately wins over the display string and yields undefined (no write). bulkActionBuildSubmitEventRecordPayload (L4587-4594) depends on this to decide whether a field was cleared. Do not 'modernise' it to `??`.; toStringArray (L1639-1641) splits plain strings on commas; multiple_choice display values containing commas inside a single option would already be split today. Preserve, do not 'fix' in wave 3.; toBooleanValue (L1545) does `String(value \|\| '')`, so numeric 0 returns undefined while '0' returns false; toNumberValue (L1562) strips every char except digits, dot, plus and minus, so '1,234.50' -> 1234.5 but '(100)' -> 100 (not -100). These are quirks consumers may rely on through buildRequestPayload; preserve.; _normalizeDateTimeTimeSegment (L1366) returns the input unchanged when unrecognised; callers chain `\|\| trimmed`. Tidying the duplicate minute branches must keep the 0..1439 bounds so a value like 2026 (a year) is not turned into a clock time.; toDateTimeRequestValueForFormat (L1241-1273) encodes Knack's rule that _raw.date is always mm/dd/yyyy regardless of field display format (CHANGELOG L38); the dd/mm swap logic must survive any refactor into lookup tables.; getFieldValueMeta is exposed through the KnackBulkActions public object (L6845, L6880) via .bind; method identity matters there, so methods must remain prototype methods (not arrow-function class fields) so `.bind(knackValueResolver)` keeps working the same way and subclass overrides still apply.; The class has no DOM or CSS side effects and no jQuery, so :visible / trigger / Chosen risks do not apply; the risk is purely data-shape regressions across 170 call sites.; Introducing static class fields instead of module-level consts avoids new page globals, but `static` fields require the V2 build target to accept ES2022 class fields; the existing esbuild/dist pipeline must not be set to a target that downlevels them into helper globals. |
| `knackValueResolver` | 1787–1787 | keep | `const knackValueResolver = new KnackValueResolver(knackNavigator)` | no | no | low | 3 | A trivial singleton whose only job is to wire the resolver to the shared navigator. It must ship in the same V2 step as the class and after knackNavigator. The real decision is ordering: either port KnackNavigator + KnackValueResolver early (they are standalone, jQuery-free, low-risk) or give wave-1 functions a module-level toStringSafe so they do not depend on this global; see crossCuttingFindings. Changes: No code change. In V2 the declaration must stay physically after `const knackNavigator = new KnackNavigator()` (V1 L797) and after the class body, because `const` is in the temporal dead zone until evaluated in a classic script.; Add a one-line JSDoc `/** Shared KnackValueResolver bound to the shared knackNavigator. @type {KnackValueResolver} */` (the brief flags jsdoc: false). Risks: 55 functions reference this global (brief calledBy), including wave-1 candidates such as validateUkMobileNumber (L13892), getConnectionRef (L6990), elementHasMeaningfulValue, hiddenInputHasMeaningfulValue and showConfirmationDialog. If those are ported to V2 before the class, V2 will throw ReferenceError at call time; tests/v2/v2-structure.test.cjs only checks declarations, not runtime references.; KnackBulkActions (L6880) and createKnackBulkActions (L6845) capture `.bind(knackValueResolver)` at load time, so the singleton must exist before those objects are evaluated in V2 as well. |

Without this, constructing the controller inside a knack-scene-render handler stacks listeners (2792-2800).; Replace the stale `@returns` at 1988 (lists 9 of 28 keys) with a `@typedef {Object} VersionRefreshController` listing every method, and add `@param {Object} [options.*]` entries for the 36 option keys that are currently undocumented.; holdRefresh (2315): keep the `hold_${Date.now()}_${n}` id scheme (consumers may log/compare it); do not switch to crypto.randomUUID(). ### C07 – Bulk actions: modal and grid controller

Lines 5092–6277 of `knackFunctions.js`.

| Function | Lines | Verdict | Current signature → proposed | Sig Δ | Shim | Risk | Wave | Plan |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `BulkActionModal` | 5096–5396 | simplify | `class BulkActionModal { constructor({ viewId, bulkActionConfig } = {}); syncOpenStateFromDom(); ensureModal(); render({ items = [], validationMessage = '' } = {}); renderActions(actions = []); setProgressState(runState = {}); open(); close(); public fields: viewId, title, emptyText, modalId, buttonClass, modalClass, isOpen, onClose, onRemove, onClear, onAction }` | no | no | low | 3 | Already modern (const, optional chaining, dataset, textContent, no jQuery) so the only gain is brevity: ensureModal is 40% of the class and is pure boilerplate that a 10-line element builder removes. Policy puts bulk actions in wave 3 with no signature changes; the public surface (constructor options, eight methods, onX callback fields, modalId) stays identical and no shim is needed. The one genuine bug-smell is the redundant appendChild in renderActions, which is harmless to delete. Changes: ensureModal (5137-5255): 120 lines of createElement/className/dataset/appendChild. Replace with a shared `el(tag, { className, dataset, text, attrs }, children)` builder or one static template literal assigned via insertAdjacentHTML (no user data goes into the static skeleton; title is set with textContent afterwards) and then wire the two listeners by querySelector. Target ~35 lines. Must emit identical class names and data-knack-bulk-basket-* attributes because bulkActionGetBaseCssText (4784-5030) and the controller's querySelectors depend on them.; Line 5181 `    body.appendChild(validation);` is mis-indented (4 spaces inside an 8-space block) - cosmetic, shows this section is not run through a formatter.; syncOpenStateFromDom (5121-5129): collapse to `const modal = document.getElementById(this.modalId); this.isOpen = Boolean(modal) && modal.style.display !== 'none';` (note: `modal?.style.display !== 'none'` would be WRONG when modal is null because undefined !== 'none' is true).; render (5262-5313): `list.innerHTML = ''` -> `list.replaceChildren()`; the four `typeof this.onX === 'function'` guards (5154, 5236, 5296, 5339) -> `this.onX?.()`; build each row through the same `el()` helper; `items.forEach` -> `for...of` or `list.append(...items.map(buildRow))`.; renderActions (5320-5349): `bulkActionCreateButton(container, ...)` already appends the button to `container` via ktl.fields.addButton, so the trailing `container.appendChild(button)` (5347) is a redundant re-append (a no-op move to end); delete it. `(Array.isArray(actions) ? actions : [])` -> early return when not an array.; setProgressState (5356-5379): the five querySelector lookups can be one `const q = (name) => modal.querySelector(`[data-knack-bulk-basket-progress-${name}="1"]`)`; `bar instanceof Element` check is redundant after querySelector (it is Element\|null) -> `if (bar) bar.style.width = ...`.; Constructor: `this.onClose = null; this.onRemove = null; ...` -> class fields `onClose = null;` etc. (ES2022 public class fields, supported in evergreen browsers).; Keep `modal.style.display = 'flex'/'none'` (5388, 5395) exactly: `.knackBulkActionModal { display: none }` at 4784-4785 and syncOpenStateFromDom both key off the inline style, and the controller's basketStore `isOpen()` callback (5618) reads `this.isOpen`. Risks: CSS in bulkActionGetBaseCssText (4784-5030) targets every class name created here (knackBulkActionModal, ...Header, ...Title, ...Close, ...Body, ...Meta, knackBulkActionValidation, ...List, ...Footer, ...Actions, ...Progress, ...ProgressSummary/Title/Percent/Detail/Track/Bar/Meta, ...Clear, ...Empty, ...Item, ...ItemText, ...ItemFailure, ...Remove, knackBulkActionButton, knackBulkActionModalAction, is-active, is-disabled, is-visible plus the summary.stateClass values). Any template rewrite must be byte-identical in class/data attribute output.; The modal lives on document.body with id knack-bulk-basket-<viewId> and survives Knack view re-renders; a new controller instance per render re-adopts it via syncOpenStateFromDom (5119). Moving it inside the view root would make it disappear on every render.; Open state is read from inline style.display (5128), not a class; switching to `hidden` or a class would break syncOpenStateFromDom and consumer CSS.; Buttons come from ktl.fields.addButton (via bulkActionCreateButton 5067) so their markup/classes (kn-button, ktlButtonMargin) are KTL's; do not replace with plain document.createElement('button') or KTL theming is lost.; Close/remove buttons use textContent 'x' with aria-label; unchanged in V2 (any upgrade to an SVG icon is a V3 visual change). |

Collapsing the mirror into store-only reads would change the observable `controller.basketItems` field, which validationCallback consumers receive via `context.controller` (5848). Keep both paths or UX changes.; getActionValidation calls consumer-supplied validationCallback(context) and treats `false`, a string, or `{valid:false, message?}` as failure; any other return (including undefined) is success. Preserve exactly - GAP-Track/Spot callbacks depend on it.; Master checkbox `indeterminate` (5962) is a property, not an attribute; keep property assignment.; clearBasket (5787-5793) relies on basketStore.clear() also wiping the stored active action key (verified in createBulkActionBasketStore.clear); if V2 swaps the store, keep that coupling. |

### C18 – Grid show/hide groups, checkboxes, postcodes

Lines 13176–13960 of `knackFunctions.js`.

| Function | Lines | Verdict | Current signature → proposed | Sig Δ | Shim | Risk | Wave | Plan |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `validateInputEmpty` | 13180–13195 | modernise | `validateInputEmpty(inputId)` | no | no | low | 1 | Old-style jQuery wrapper with no internal callers (brief) and 16 lines; a direct native rewrite removes jQuery and the removeClassFromSelector coupling. Boolean return and class side effects are unchanged, so no shim. Wave 1 because it is small, jQuery-only and low risk. Changes: Replace `$(`#${inputId}`)` (13181) with `document.getElementById(inputId)` routed through `resolveElement` so an Element or jQuery input keeps working.; Replace the blur handler (13183-13187) with `input.addEventListener('blur', () => input.classList.toggle('inputInvalid', !input.value))` and drop the jQuery-based `removeClassFromSelector` dependency (line 9645).; Bind the blur listener once per element (dataset flag / proposed bindOnce) - jQuery `.on` at 13183 stacks a new handler on every call from a render handler.; Replace `input.addClass('inputInvalid').focus(); input[0].scrollIntoView(...)` (13190-13191) with `classList.add`, `focus({ preventScroll: true })` and `scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' })`, returning false early when the element is missing.; Keep the `!input.value` test (whitespace counts as a value) rather than trimming, to avoid a behaviour change. Risks: jQuery `.val()` on a `<select multiple>` returns an array (an empty array is truthy) and on a checkbox returns the value attribute regardless of checked state; native `.value` differs for multi-selects. Only text inputs/textareas are realistic callers, but document it.; The `inputInvalid` class is styled by consumer CSS, not by this library; the class name must stay exactly as is.; Current code throws when the element is missing (13191); returning false instead removes a thrown error - treat as a bug fix and report via errorHandler.; jQuery `.focus()` runs jQuery focus handlers synchronously; native `.focus()` still reaches Knack's jQuery-delegated handlers because the native focus event bubbles through jQuery's delegation. |

No internal callers (brief) but the global is public. Wave 1 because it is a small, low-risk simplification with no signature change. Changes: Replace the manual header scan (13207-13211) plus `getAllTableRows` + `row.children[colIndex]` (13213-13217) with one query: `table.querySelectorAll(`tbody tr:not(.kn-table-group) td.${fieldClass}`).forEach(td => td.classList.add(CLASS_DISABLED))` - Knack body cells carry the same `field_x` class as their header.; Accept both `123` and `'field_123'` via `knackNavigator.normalizeFieldId(colHeadID)` (additive; today only a bare number works because of the `field_${colHeadID}` template at 13210).; Accept `viewId` with or without the `view_` prefix via `knackNavigator.normalizeViewId` / the proposed getGridTable primitive (13203-13206).; Fix the JSDoc, which documents a `doRestyle` parameter (13200) that does not exist.; If an index approach is retained, reuse `getIndexOfColumnHeader(viewElement, colHeadID)` (line 9057) instead of re-implementing it. Risks: The index loop at 13209 keeps overwriting, so it matches the LAST header cell with the class; a `td.field_x` query disables every matching cell - identical unless a field appears twice in one grid.; Index arithmetic mis-targets when a row has fewer cells than the header (`.kn-tr-nodata` rows are not excluded by getAllTableRows); a class-based query cannot.; `CLASS_DISABLED` is the literal 'disabled' (line 9); consumer CSS may key on it, keep the constant. |
| `hideEmptyGridColumns` | 13229–13249 | keep | `hideEmptyGridColumns(viewId, fieldIds)` | no | no | low | 2 | Recent native code (CHANGELOG) that already matches the V2 targets; nothing to gain beyond a one-line input widening. Wave 2 so it is ported together with the other grid utilities and the knackNavigator column helpers it depends on. Changes: Accept a single field id as well as an array (`[].concat(fieldIds)`) - additive to the `Array.isArray` guard at 13232.; Reuse the proposed `getGridTable(viewId)` primitive for the view/table lookup (13230-13233).; Consider `CLASS_DISPLAY_NONE` instead of inline `style.display = 'none'` (13247) so a column can be re-shown by class removal; the function is new (CHANGELOG line 6) so inline-style dependence by consumers is unlikely, but verify before changing. Risks: Emptiness is `textContent.trim() === ''` (13239): cells containing only an image, icon, file thumbnail or an unchecked boolean rendering count as empty and the whole column disappears. Document rather than change.; `colgroup col:nth-child(n)` (13245) assumes the colgroup matches the header count; `addCheckboxes` inserts a `th` without a `col`, so the hidden `col` can be the neighbour's. `getRenderedViewColumnIndex` (line 415) is DOM-based so the th/td part is correct.; The hide is lost on every Knack re-render (paging/sort/inline edit) and must be re-applied from `knack-view-render`, as the JSDoc says. |

### C20 – KnackAPI and API usage

Lines 14161–17119 of `knackFunctions.js`.

| Function | Lines | Verdict | Current signature → proposed | Sig Δ | Shim | Risk | Wave | Plan |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `KnackAPI` | 14161–17047 | simplify | `class KnackAPI { static get DEFAULT_WRITE_CONCURRENCY (6); constructor(options = {}); getRecords(sceneId, viewId, options = {}); getAllRecords(sceneId, viewId, options = {}); getRecordChildren(sceneId, viewId, recordId, parentSceneSlug, options = {}); getAllRecordChildren(sceneId, viewId, recordId, parentSceneSlug, options = {}); createRecord(sceneId, viewId, recordData, options = {}); createRecords(sceneId, viewId, recordsData, options = {}); uploadAsset(file, options = {}); updateRecord(sceneId, viewId, recordId, recordData, options = {}); updateRecords(sceneId, viewId, recordIds, recordData, options = {}); updateRecordsWithDelay(sceneId, viewId, recordIds, recordData, options = {}); deleteRecord(sceneId, viewId, recordId, options = {}); deleteRecords(sceneId, viewId, recordIds, options = {}); refreshView(viewId); getRecord(sceneId, viewId, recordId); forceLog(message, data); isDeveloper(); setDebug(enabled); setWriteConcurrency(writeConcurrency); getApiUsageStats(); getRunApiCallCount(); getRateLimitStatus(); resetApiUsageStats(options = {}); refreshApiUsageStats(sceneId, viewId, options = {}); logApiUsageStats(options = {}); buildFilters(filters); buildSorters(sorters); formatConnectedFields(records, connectedFields); plus ~65 underscore-prefixed private methods }` | no | no | high | 3 | Already ES2020+ async code with no jQuery, so it meets the wave-3 "port as-is with dead-code removal and consistency fixes" rule and must not change signatures: it is the write path for bulk actions (4356, 6453-6507), KnackError (21804, 22819), the version refresh controller (2066), quick toggles (21278, 21697) and two older helpers (12157-12167, 13120-13145). The savings are real but mechanical: four dead methods, one unreachable throw, two copy-pasted pagination loops, three copy-pasted batch scaffolds and a duplicated header builder, plus two genuine bugs (relative upload URL, verification against the un-prepared payload). Risk is high because the rate limiter, retry/error shapes and Backbone-based refresh are production-critical and only testable against a live app. Changes: Delete dead private methods with no callers anywhere in the file: _createAbortController (15835-15847), _handleResponse (15886-15893) and _executeRequest (15903-15905; it also passes a timeout only when signal is absent, which is nonsensical). Keep _scheduleWrite (16628-16630) as a one-line @deprecated alias because its JSDoc promises backward compatibility for "older internals"; delete it in V3.; Remove the unreachable `throw new Error('Max retries exceeded')` at 16902: every path through the for-loop at 16845-16897 either returns outcome.result or throws normalizedError.; Extract a private `_fetchAllPages(fetchPage, { rows, pageConcurrency, failOnPageError, onProgress, timeout, filters, sorters }, logContext)`: getAllRecords (14256-14305) and getAllRecordChildren (14373-14427) are line-for-line identical apart from the page fetcher and the log label (~55 lines saved).; Extract a private `_runBatchWrite({ operation, code, target, items, execute, options })` that owns the counters, the `_buildBatchContext` call, the try/finally with `_logBatchSummary`, the `_refreshAfterWrite` + `_logBatchFailures` + rethrow-of-firstError tail: createRecords (14509-14575), updateRecords (14923-15006) and deleteRecords (15094-15158) repeat that scaffold three times (~90 lines saved). Keep the three public result shapes ({created}\|{updated}\|{deleted}) by passing the counter key name.; Merge _buildUploadHeaders (15963-15977) into `_buildHeaders(withAuth = true, { json = true } = {})`; the only difference is the Content-Type line. _requestInner (16770) and uploadAsset (14611) call the merged helper.; Bug: uploadAsset builds its URL from `String(Knack?.api_dev \|\| '')` at 14600, so when api_dev is absent the request goes to the relative path `/applications/...`; use `this._getApiBaseUrl()` (17033-17035) like every other request.; Bug: updateRecord verification (14870-14893) iterates `Object.keys(recordData)` and compares `recordData[key]` (the un-prepared payload, which holds a Blob when autoUploadAssets is on) with the response; use `preparedRecordData` so asset updates stop logging a spurious "Field update verification: failures detected". Also log `preparedRecordData` rather than the Blob at 14857.; JSDoc correction: createRecords/updateRecords/deleteRecords document `[options.continueOnError=false]` (14500, 14916, 15090) but _buildBatchContext sets `continueOnError = opts.continueOnError !== false` (16474), so the real default is true. V2 keeps the true default (bulk actions at 6453-6462 depend on partial success) and fixes the three doc lines.; getRecord (15203-15218): replace the `console.error` + rethrow at 15215 with `this._log(..., 'error')` + rethrow so it respects the debug/developer gate like every other method. Keep the three-parameter signature; an optional trailing options object is a V3 candidate (document in tests/v2/signature-exceptions.json if added earlier).; _log (15781-15795): `data \|\| ''` hides falsy data such as 0/false; use `data ?? ''` and collapse the switch to `console[level === 'warn' \|\| level === 'error' ? level : 'log'](prefix, data ?? '')`.; Constructor (14179-14216): extract `resolveRetryOptions(options)` so the three nested ternaries (14185-14199) become a flat lookup that documents both option spellings in JSDoc (currently only retry429* is documented at 14174-14176 while the code prefers maxRetries/retryDelayBase/retryDelayMax).; _refreshSingleView (16119-16213): convert from a hand-rolled Promise with try/catch to `async`, wrap only the Backbone `view.model.fetch` callback pair in a Promise, and replace the four `typeof view.X === 'function' ? call : reject` blocks with a lookup table {form: 'reloadForm', calendar: 'renderRecords', search: 'renderResults', menu: 'postRender'}. Preserve the per-type side calls exactly (form also calls view.render(); calendar also calls Knack.hideSpinner?.(); search first triggers model change and renderForm/renderView).; _formatParams (16099-16113): `new URLSearchParams(params)` accepts a plain object directly, so the body is `return Object.keys(params ?? {}).length ? `?${new URLSearchParams(params)}` : ''`.; _normalizeResponseHeaders (15531-15547): `Object.fromEntries(Array.from(headersLike.entries?.() ?? Object.entries(headersLike), ([k, v]) => [String(k).toLowerCase(), v]))`.; Use `Object.hasOwn` at 16257 (and in the resolver helper it calls) instead of `Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call`.; _buildBatchContext (16471-16494) spreads every batch option (onProgress, staggerMs, requireRecordId, continueOnError) into the per-record `requestOptions`; pass only the keys the single-record methods read ({ timeout, autoUploadAssets, assetFieldIds, assetTypesByField, onAssetProgress, _on429 }).; Share the inline JSON-or-text parse IIFE (14697-14703 and 16788-16794) and the `new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms))` sleeps (15035, 16546, 16895) via the module-level `parseJsonOrText` and `sleep` primitives proposed below.; Add the missing JSDoc on _normaliseDateTimeForCompare (16306), _normaliseDisplayForCompare (16347) and _valuesEffectivelyEqualForField (16364); normalise the mis-indented @param/@example lines (14174, 14340-14341, 14451-14461, 14836-14844, 15785).; updateRecordsWithDelay (15023-15043): keep the signature and sequential, error-collecting semantics (it returns per-record results including {error, recordId}, which updateRecords does not), but add `@deprecated Use updateRecords(..., { staggerMs, continueOnError: true })` and run `this._assertWriteOptions(options, 'updateRecordsWithDelay')` for consistency with the other writers.; Replace the stale class header `@version 1.0.1` (14159) and the `//*************** Helpers ...` banner (16240) with a plain JSDoc section comment; the CHANGELOG already tracks versions.; Spelling: the class mixes _normalizeRefreshViews/_normalizeResponseHeaders/_normalizeExternalRateLimitSnapshot (US) with _normaliseForCompare/_normaliseDateTimeForCompare (UK). Do not rename in wave 3 (private methods are still reachable on a public class instance); any NEW helper uses UK spelling. Risks: Write-queue semantics (16397-16720): default concurrency 6, 9 dispatches per rolling second, drop to writeMinConcurrency and pause on 429, ramp +1 per completed job once writeRampDelayMs has elapsed, `Infinity` bypass at 16612 that still defers the task to a microtask. Bulk actions (6453-6462) and the version refresh controller depend on this ordering and throttling; a refactor that changes dispatch order or the per-second window will surface as 429 storms in production.; _request (16723-16747) deduplicates in-flight GETs by `url::timeout`; two callers share one promise, so a timeout or abort affects both. Keep the key format and the finally-delete.; Non-OK responses are thrown as plain objects {status, statusText, responseText, headers} (16777-16782, 14707-14712) and normalised by _buildRequestError (16911-16934) into an Error with .status, .body and the message `API error ${status}: ${message}`; KnackError and bulk actions may inspect .status/.body or match the message text. Keep both the intermediate shape (used for Retry-After via error.headers.get at 16987) and the final Error shape.; continueOnError defaults to true (16474) despite the JSDoc saying false; batch methods only rethrow firstError when it is explicitly false. Flipping the default to match the docs would make bulk replication abort on the first failure.; _runBatchWorkers (16531-16584): when continueOnError is false, scheduling stops but already-running workers finish, so counts can exceed the failing index; tests must not assume an exact stop.; refreshView/_refreshSingleView (15184-15196, 16119-16213) rely on undocumented Backbone internals (view.model.fetch, reloadForm, renderRecords, renderResults, postRender, model.trigger('change')); view types outside {form, calendar, search, menu, details, table} (list, map, report) fetch but never re-render (16179-16195) and resolve silently. Port verbatim; any "improvement" needs a live-app check. Unverified against live docs.; Logging gate: _log prints only when options.debug AND (developer role OR forceLog) (15782), so forceLog() still needs debug: true. _initLogSettings (15983-16004) calls Knack.getUserRoleNames() in the constructor; a client constructed before Knack is ready logs a console.warn (16001) and never shows logs. Keep the gate exactly or non-developer consoles get spammed.; Rate-limit snapshot header names (x-planlimit-*, x-rate-limit-*, 15616-15618) are speculative; browsers only expose CORS-safelisted headers unless Knack sends Access-Control-Expose-Headers, which the code's own fallback reason strings (15624-15626) admit. refreshApiUsageStats also POSTs the user session token to http://localhost:3001 by default (15358, 15661-15720). Unverified against live docs.; uploadAsset uses XMLHttpRequest (14673-14741) specifically for upload progress events; fetch has no upload progress, so do not "modernise" it to fetch. The endpoint `/applications/{id}/assets/{type}/upload` with view-based auth headers is undocumented (unverified against live docs).; The class reads the top-level `const knackNavigator` (797) and `const knackValueResolver` (1787) at call time (14810-14816, 16251-16252, 16350, 16365-16366); V2 must declare those instances before any KnackAPI instance is constructed (KnackError constructs one at 21804, getKnackApiClient lazily) or hit the temporal dead zone.; Error reporting deliberately uses console via _log rather than errorHandler.handle: KnackError (21772) wraps a KnackAPI to write error records, so routing KnackAPI failures into errorHandler would recurse (KnackError -> createRecord -> 429 -> errorHandler -> KnackError). Keep console-based logging here as a documented exception to the policy.; buildFilters (16011-16056) accepts both the documented `{match, rules}` JSON form (16016-16021) and a bracket form `filters[0][field]=...` (16029-16055) that the view-based records API probably ignores (same failure class as the sort[0][field] bug recorded in CHANGELOG line 29). Converting the bracket form to JSON is a behaviour change for any caller that currently passes an array and silently gets unfiltered results; do it only as a deliberate, changelog-noted fix after a live test. Unverified against live docs. |

### C21 – Conditional required fields, notifications, inputs

Lines 17441–17635, 17871–18258 of `knackFunctions.js`.

[]`, and read `globalThis.Knack?.views` (bare `Knack?.views` at 17474 still throws ReferenceError when Knack is undeclared, e.g. in the Playwright harness).; The closure hasVisibleInputValue (17491-17519) re-implements fieldWrapperHasValue (17409-17437) line for line, adding only the datetime branch at 17496-17502 and dropping the hidden-input check at 17432-17436. The behaviour (what counts as cleared, when events fire) is subtle enough that the rewrite must be differential-tested, hence medium risk, but it is self-contained, 68 lines, no widget coupling beyond one Chosen trigger, so wave 1. Changes: Resolve containers with `resolveElements(inputContainer)` (18031) and collect controls with `el.matches('input, select, textarea') ? [el] : [...el.querySelectorAll('input, select, textarea')]`; drop the impossible-state guards `if (!field) return` (18034), `field.matches &&` (18036), `if (!input) return` (18045) and `field.querySelectorAll ? ... : []` (18057-18059).; Extract the radio-group resolution (18047-18064) into the shared `getRadioGroup(radioInput, root)` primitive (same-name radios within the container, falling back to document, falling back to [input]) and keep the per-container `processedRadioGroups` Set.; Replace the jQuery block at 18080-18085 (`$(targetInput).data('chosen')` + `.trigger('liszt:updated')`) with the shared `refreshChosenSelect(select)` primitive: native detection via `isChosenSelect` (next sibling `.chzn-container`/`.chosen-container`, as addInputEventListener already does at 7976-7986) and a single jQuery trigger, which is the policy's permitted Chosen exception. Also removes the bare global `$` (18081) that is used while only `window.jQuery` was checked (18080).; Factor the per-control reset into a small `resetControl(control)` returning whether it had a value, so the hadValue/clear/dispatch sequence (18067-18093) reads as three lines.; Replace `Array.from(targetInput.options).forEach(option => option.selected = false)` (18075) with `for (const o of select.options) o.selected = false` and keep `selectedIndex = -1` for single selects (that is V1's observable result: no option selected, not the first option). Risks: Single selects end with selectedIndex -1 (18077), i.e. no selected option, which Chosen renders as its placeholder; do not change to `selectedIndex = 0` (that is resetSelectToFirstOption's job).; `input, select, textarea` includes `input[type=hidden]`; clearing hidden inputs matters for Knack connection pickers that keep their value in a hidden input - V1 clears them to '' and V2 must too, otherwise the visible Chosen resets but the submitted value does not.; The Chosen refresh is only attempted when the select is Chosen-enhanced; V1 detected that via jQuery `.data('chosen')` (Chosen 0.9.x stores its instance there). Native sibling detection is equivalent for Knack's markup but would miss a Chosen instance whose container was moved; firing `liszt:updated` unconditionally on every select is the safe fallback (it is a no-op without Chosen).; Events are dispatched natively (18088-18089); jQuery and Knack/Backbone handlers do receive native bubbling `input`/`change` events, so this already works in V1 and is unchanged.; Datepicker/timepicker inputs are cleared by `value = ''` without telling the widget; the jQuery UI datepicker re-reads the input on open, so this is fine, but a consumer expecting `onSelect` will not get it (same as V1).; `triggerChange && hadValue` means a select whose first option has value '' never emits change even if its selectedIndex changed from 0 to -1; preserve. |

### C22 – Detail values, scene, user and navigation

Lines 18259–18890 of `knackFunctions.js`.

| Function | Lines | Verdict | Current signature → proposed | Sig Δ | Shim | Risk | Wave | Plan |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `waitGetValueFromDetail` | 18265–18324 | simplify | `async function waitGetValueFromDetail({viewId, fieldIds, delay = 20000, returnHtml = false})` | no | no | medium | 1 | Already async/await and jQuery-free, but 60 lines carry a real defect (the timeout option is never applied, L18282-18285), an unreachable 12-line catch block and a hand-rolled object builder. Same signature and return types, so no shim; the only observable change is the corrected wait time, which must be called out. It belongs in wave 1 because it shares primitives with getValueFromDetail and the connection-id helpers in this chunk. Changes: L18282-18285: `waitSelector({selector, delay})` - waitSelector (L8772-8777) destructures `timeout`, not `delay`, so the option is silently discarded and every call waits waitSelector's default 10000 ms. Pass `timeout: delay`; the warning at L18288 (`within ${delay}ms`) then stops lying.; L18269-18304: replace the mutable `fieldValues` object, the `fieldStatuses` intermediate and the forEach with `Object.fromEntries(await Promise.all(fieldIdArray.map(async (id) => { const key = knackNavigator.normalizeFieldId(id); return [key, await readOne(key)]; })))`.; L18271 + L18312-18323: the outer try/catch is unreachable. The inner try/catch (L18281-18290) turns timeouts into `{found:false}`, Promise.all over never-rejecting promises cannot reject, and knackNavigator.normalize* never throw. Delete it; the recovery loop at L18316-18320 also keys by the raw un-normalised fieldId so it could never have matched the normalised keys.; L18296-18303: use the shared `readElementContent(element, returnHtml)` primitive and apply `\|\| null` here, so this function and getValueFromDetail trim identically.; L18277-18279: build the selector with the shared `detailBodySelector(fieldId, {viewId, inner: true})` so the `#view_x .field_y .kn-detail-body span` string exists once (the same string is hand-built at L12279-12280).; L18307-18308: single return `Array.isArray(fieldIds) ? values : values[knackNavigator.normalizeFieldId(fieldIds)]`.; Keep `console.warn` for a timed-out field (an absent field is an expected state on pages that do not render it); do not route it through errorHandler. Risks: Fixing `delay` -> `timeout` changes real timing: a call that gives up after 10 s today will wait the documented 20 s by default, and `delay: 5000` callers will become faster. CHANGELOG L92 shows consumers call this directly, so note it in the V2 changelog.; `.kn-detail-body span` only matches when Knack wraps the value in a <span>; bodies rendered without one (empty values, some paragraph/rich-text fields - unverified against live docs) resolve to null even though `.kn-detail-body` has text. Port the selector verbatim.; Empty string values return null (`'' \|\| null`, L18298-18299) whereas getValueFromDetail returns ''. Preserve the asymmetry.; The result object is keyed by normalised ids (`field_12`) even when the caller passed 12 (L18286). Preserve.; waitSelector observes `attributes` on the whole body subtree (L8879-8883), so this is expensive on large grids; that is waitSelector's problem but a V2 that calls it N times for N fields should consider one observer. |
